        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath.json",
          "text": "What is the Beneath?",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "What is the Beneath?",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath.json",
          "text": "The Beneath is a network of very deep caves, directly below the Overworld. This dimension is balanced around mid to late Primitive Age. It's more dangerous than the surface, so we recommend a full set of bronze armor and a metal shield as a minimum, as well as plenty of food, water, and other tools.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "What is the Beneath?",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath.json",
          "text": "It differs from the usual Nether experience in a few ways: - You enter it differently - Water doesn't instantly evaporate - A bed works like normal - Traveling Beneath is no faster than in the Overworld In return, you can find resources like Kaolin Clay, Graphite, richer and more exposed ore veins, as well as other treasures.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "What is the Beneath?",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath.json",
          "text": "Entering the Beneath",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "What is the Beneath?",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath.json",
          "text": "To travel Beneath, simply stand on overworld bedrock for a little while. To get down to the bedrock, you'll have to either break through the Hornfels layer with Explosives or Steel+ Tools, or find a cave with the more brittle Keratophyre.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "What is the Beneath?",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath.json",
          "text": "To return, stand directly below ceiling bedrock in the same fashion. It's a good idea to make a note of where you entered, so you can return to the same place in the overworld whenever you're ready to leave.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "What is the Beneath?",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "minecraft:deepslate",
          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "(SiO₂)₄(KMg₃Al₃F₂Si₃O₁₀)",
          "key": "runtime-tooltip",
          "label": "Raw Migmatite",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Ores and Minerals",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "The ores you can find Beneath are mostly similar to what you can find on the surface, but with a few changes. Consult the Ores in TFG category for exact details.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "The most common rock type found is Migmatite. This deep metamorphic rock contains a wide variety of ores you'd normally find in other rock types on the surface.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "In wetter areas, you may find Travertine. It is a sedimentary rock that can contain the same kind of ores as Migmatite.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Around the deepest level of the Beneath, the different rock types gradually all turn into Pyroxenite. This ultramafic igneous rock is extremely rich in ores, but this depth also comes with extreme heat, so be prepared!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Anthracite is a powerful fuel. It can be found in Migmatite in the upper half of the Beneath.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Ores and Minerals",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Beneath the overworld, you will still find ore veins to exploit. It is important to note that Netherrack does not collapse, like raw stone would. However, Blackstone, Nether Bricks, Cobblerack and miscellaneous other blocks do. Prospecting will still work Beneath the overworld.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Nether Gold",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Nether Gold replaces netherrack, and is common. Beneath lava, there is more gold, and richer.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Cursecoal is a powerful fuel. It replaces netherrack above level 80.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Sylvite replaces basalt at any level.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Nether Quartz",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Nether Quartz replaces netherrack at all levels, and is common.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Slimed Netherrack",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Slimed Netherrack occasionally replaces Netherrack above lava level.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/nether_vein_index",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/nether_vein_index.json",
          "text": "The Beneath Vein Index",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "The Beneath Vein Index",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 180
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "text": "This is the Vein Index for The Beneath. Each Vein has details regarding its rarity, density, vein type, height found, sizes, what stones it spawns in, and more.",

        {

        {

        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "text": "- Saltpeter, Diatomite... - Scheelite, Tungstate, Lithium - Soapstone, Talc... - Sulfur, Pyrite, Sphalerite - Sulfur, Sphalerite, Pyrite - Tetrahedrite, Native Copper... - Wulfenite, Molybdenite...",

        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "First, like the Moon, Mars will have some hostile surface mobs as well. If you haven't already figured out how to make your Space Suit stay with you after dying or invested in new EV-tier weaponry, you'll have a rough time.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The planet is mostly a desert world, dotted with \"islands\" where you’ll find water, lush fauna, and much safer places to build your first base. Meanwhile, the open desert is extremely dangerous if you don't watch your step, but contains some other crucial resources!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "{@pagebreak}",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "All fluid veins are spread across Mars, except for Heavy Ammoniacial Water which can only be found outside of the desert. That’s another good reason to set up your base there.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Energy-wise, Mars will not be kind to you at first.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Nearly every common energy generation method is disabled or worthless on the planet, which means you’ll have to rely almost entirely on Fission to power your base.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "At the very beginning, we recommend bringing along some filled batteries such as Lapotron Crystals to get started, and then look towards setting up Thorium Fuel Rods.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Achieving energy stability on Mars will take time and effort, but once your systems are running smoothly, you’ll feel like unlimited power is right in the palm of your hand.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "And one last tip: don’t kill everything you see. Some animals can be ranched, and you’ll definitely need them later in your progression.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Be sure to check out the Space Survival chapter for more Mars-related tips too!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Stay alert, plan ahead, and Mars will reward your courage.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The Red Planet",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Land on Mars",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "I'm on Mars!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "one_completed",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The water on Mars is Semiheavy which means it contains one Deuterium atom, and is also full of Ammonia, a great antifreeze, which is how it can stay liquid despite the very low temperatures.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "It's technically drinkable, but you'll likely want to distill it into normal Water first. You can also move source blocks of Semiheavy Ammoniacal Water around and get more of it via an Aqueous Accumulator, just like regular Water.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "You can also centrifuge the Ammonium Chloride out from it and electrolyze it as a source of Nitrogen. Easy Nitrox from just one source!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Fancy a drink?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Semiheavy Ammoniacal Water",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Dotted around the vast Martian deserts are small \"islands\" of life and vegetation, which are probably where you'll want to set up your base. Surviving here will be much easier than in the desert!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Is that a dinosaur?!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The Martian Jungles",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The majority of Mars is covered in a vast desert. Not much lives here except the occasional Stackatick, and of course the giant Sandworms. If this is your first time here, you'll want to find somewhere more hospitable! Otherwise, you'll want to keep searching...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Sand, sand everywhere",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Visit the Martian Deep Desert biome",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The Martian Desert",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "If you make too much noise in the desert, you might attract the attention of the mighty Sandworm! It's more of an environmental hazard than a boss to kill, so your best way of surviving it is to run away!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "If you manage to hit its head enough times with explosion damage, it will leave you alone... for a while.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Thankfully, the Sandworm can only damage entities, not buildings, and is only attracted to the footsteps of players, so any way to keep your own feet off the ground won't anger it. The atmosphere is too thin for airplanes and hang gliders, but there are other tools...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "SHAI-HULUD",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The Sandworm",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Ostrum Deposits are immovable blocks that you can only find in the Martian Deep Desert, far away from any of the more lush areas. They spawn in small blobs, and can be broken to make space for your Ostrum Harvester if they're on a slope. Try using a Spyglass to find them from a distance!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "You'll have to figure out some long-distance logistics to ship your Ostrum back to your base. (Try a train!)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Ostrum is a crucial resource for both EV progression and making infinite ores from Mars.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Spice Melange",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The spice must flow",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Ostrum Deposits",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Is Mars not cold enough for you?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Head up (or down) towards one of its poles at Z=+15000/-5000. If anything's able to live there, surely it must have amazing insulative properties...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Cold enough for CO₂ to freeze!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The Martian Poles",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Heavy Ammoniacal Water is another kind of fluid that can only be found deep underground via a Fluid Drilling Rig. You can only find it in these lush areas of Mars, not the desert!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "You can separate it into Heavy Water, which is important for Fission power.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The Cooler Ammoniacal Water",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Heavy Ammoniacal Water",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Unlike regular Sheep on Earth, killing a Glacian one will only give you normal Wool. You'll need to build up their familiarity and shear them if you want the special Glacian Wool.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "You'll need 7 of it for a full Tungstensteel Space Suit, but you can also use it as a great component for your Fission Reactor to increase its maximum heat.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "You can also turn it into regular Wool Yarn and Cloth if you want more of that for some reason.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "What an incredible insulator!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Glacian Wool",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Airplanes don't work on Mars, and your space suit doesn't have a jetpack, so there's no other way to fly, right?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "If you're able to find an elusive Soarer, you can tame them with Raw Fish and equip them with a Saddle to glide through the Martian skies with ease.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "They can't fly forever though, indicated by the wing icons on the top left, and will need to land occasionally. (You can feed them more Raw Fish to speed up their recovery.)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Still, they're a great option for long-distance travel, and won't upset the sandworm either!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Sniffers and Wraptors can be found all over Mars and can be domesticated for meat, eggs, and most importantly, their mineral-rich tufts and feathers.",

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/moon.snbt",
          "text": "Chorus Fruit serves a pretty crucial role on the Moon - it will be your main source of Nitrogen. To get started, find some Chorus Plants, parkour up to the top, and break the Chorus Flowers. Breaking the plant from the bottom will not get you any of the Chorus Flowers! Next up, grow the Chorus Flowers in an Electric Greenhouse to get a renewable source of Chorus Fruit, and from there, you can process them into Biomass and Nitrogen with a Brewery and Fermenter respectively.           "text": "1) If you haven't set up any stone-related automation before, here's a quick introduction: A Rock Crusher can produce infinite raw stone, which a Macerator crushes into dust for a Centrifuge to process.",

          "text": "This is a list of every huge mushroom on Mars, and what climates they grow in. Looking for particular type of mushroom stem to decorate your base? This should help you find it. The list is sorted alphabetically by stem type. Also check out the Earth Tree Index.",

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "There are many, many, many different types of plants in TerraFirmaCraft. Different plants appear in different Climates, and their appearance may change over the current season - going through cycles of flowering and laying dormant, or changing color as the local temperature changes. Colorful flowers can typically be crushed in a Quern for Dye.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Standard plants are like small flowers. They grow on grass, dirt, and farmland.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Dry plants are like standard plants, but they can grow on sand. These generally only spawn in areas with low rainfall.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Cacti can grow two blocks high, and they will damage you!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Creeping plants take the shape of the surrounding block faces. They mostly spawn in blobs on the ground",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Epiphytes only live on the sides of logs.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Short Grass",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.name",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Short grass blocks grow taller with age. They also are able to grow on peat and mud.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Tall Grass",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.name",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Tall grass blocks are just tall enough to block your field of view.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Vine blocks spread around the world on their own, if it's warm enough.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Weeping Vines",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.name",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Weeping vines grow downward from an anchoring block.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Twisting Vines",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.name",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Twisting vines twist upward from the Earth. They can come in a solid variant.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Water plants are restricted to either spawn in fresh or salty water. Otherwise, they grow and act much like the plants you see on the surface. Some water plants can be cooked for food.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Water Plants",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.title",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Standard Water",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.name",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Standard water plants can be broken with a Knife to get Seaweed.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Water Grass",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.name",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Water grasses are grasses that grow under the water.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Tall Water Plant",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.name",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Tall water plants can grow with just the bottom block in water. Water Taro and Cattail can be broken with a Knife for Roots.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Floating plants sit on top of the water. Boats will break them on contact.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.15.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Kelp are twisting vines that grow underwater.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.16.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Tree Kelp",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.17.name",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Tree kelp grow into intricate trees underwater. The flowers can be harvested with a Knife.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.17.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },

    {
      "id": "pack:tfg/ores",
      "label": "Ores",
      "origin": "pack_generated",
      "confidence": 0.75,
      "support": 9596,
      "evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "item_tag",
          "id": "balm:ores",

        },
        {
          "kind": "item_tag",
          "id": "forge:ores",

        }
      ],
      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Ores and Minerals",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "The ores you can find Beneath are mostly similar to what you can find on the surface, but with a few changes. Consult the Ores in TFG category for exact details.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "The most common rock type found is Migmatite. This deep metamorphic rock contains a wide variety of ores you'd normally find in other rock types on the surface.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "In wetter areas, you may find Travertine. It is a sedimentary rock that can contain the same kind of ores as Migmatite.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Around the deepest level of the Beneath, the different rock types gradually all turn into Pyroxenite. This ultramafic igneous rock is extremely rich in ores, but this depth also comes with extreme heat, so be prepared!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Anthracite is a powerful fuel. It can be found in Migmatite in the upper half of the Beneath.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Ores and Minerals",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Beneath the overworld, you will still find ore veins to exploit. It is important to note that Netherrack does not collapse, like raw stone would. However, Blackstone, Nether Bricks, Cobblerack and miscellaneous other blocks do. Prospecting will still work Beneath the overworld.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Nether Gold",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Nether Gold replaces netherrack, and is common. Beneath lava, there is more gold, and richer.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Cursecoal is a powerful fuel. It replaces netherrack above level 80.",

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "advancedperipherals:manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/husbandry_automata_core",
          "source": "jar:AdvancedPeripherals-1.20.1-0.7.46r.jar!assets/advancedperipherals/patchouli_books/manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/husbandry_automata_core.json",
          "text": "Husbandry automata core",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Husbandry automata core",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "advancedperipherals:manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/husbandry_automata_core",
          "source": "jar:AdvancedPeripherals-1.20.1-0.7.46r.jar!assets/advancedperipherals/patchouli_books/manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/husbandry_automata_core.json",
          "text": "Husbandry automata core",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Husbandry automata core",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "advancedperipherals:manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/husbandry_automata_core",
          "source": "jar:AdvancedPeripherals-1.20.1-0.7.46r.jar!assets/advancedperipherals/patchouli_books/manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/husbandry_automata_core.json",
          "text": "After consuming 3 chickens, 3 cows, 3 sheeps, weak automata core will be transformed to husbandry automata core!In addition to be more powerful weak automata core variant, seems, this soul also provide abilities to interact with animals!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Husbandry automata core",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "advancedperipherals:manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/husbandry_automata_core",
          "source": "jar:AdvancedPeripherals-1.20.1-0.7.46r.jar!assets/advancedperipherals/patchouli_books/manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/husbandry_automata_core.json",
          "text": "In additions to weak automata core abilities, this core also allow to interact with animals and even transfer them inside!But be aware, any stored animal will be lost after turtle is broken.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Husbandry automata core",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "advancedperipherals:husbandry_automata_core",
          "source": "jar:AdvancedPeripherals-1.20.1-0.7.46r.jar!data/advancedperipherals/advancements/husbandry_automata_core.json",
          "text": "Husbandry automata core",
          "key": "advancement-title:title",
          "label": "Husbandry automata core",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "advancedperipherals:husbandry_automata_core",
          "source": "jar:AdvancedPeripherals-1.20.1-0.7.46r.jar!data/advancedperipherals/advancements/husbandry_automata_core.json",
          "text": "Is this core gluten-free?",
          "key": "advancement-description:description",
          "label": "Husbandry automata core",

        },
        {

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "advancedperipherals:manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/end_automata_core",
          "source": "jar:AdvancedPeripherals-1.20.1-0.7.46r.jar!assets/advancedperipherals/patchouli_books/manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/end_automata_core.json",
          "text": "End automata core",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "End automata core",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "advancedperipherals:manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/end_automata_core",
          "source": "jar:AdvancedPeripherals-1.20.1-0.7.46r.jar!assets/advancedperipherals/patchouli_books/manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/end_automata_core.json",
          "text": "End automata core",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "End automata core",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "advancedperipherals:manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/end_automata_core",
          "source": "jar:AdvancedPeripherals-1.20.1-0.7.46r.jar!assets/advancedperipherals/patchouli_books/manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/end_automata_core.json",
          "text": "After consuming 10 endermans, weak automata core will be transformed to end automata core!In addition to be more powerful weak automata core variant, seems, this soul also provide some limited teleportation abilities for turtle itself.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "End automata core",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "advancedperipherals:manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/end_automata_core",
          "source": "jar:AdvancedPeripherals-1.20.1-0.7.46r.jar!assets/advancedperipherals/patchouli_books/manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/end_automata_core.json",
          "text": "In additions to weak automata core abilities, this soul also allow limited world-bound teleportation. Seems, you need to store points and then teleport to them!But be aware, any stored points will be lost after turtle is broken.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "End automata core",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "advancedperipherals:manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/fuel_consuming",
          "source": "jar:AdvancedPeripherals-1.20.1-0.7.46r.jar!assets/advancedperipherals/patchouli_books/manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/fuel_consuming.json",
          "text": "Cooldowns and fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Cooldowns and fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "advancedperipherals:manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/fuel_consuming",
          "source": "jar:AdvancedPeripherals-1.20.1-0.7.46r.jar!assets/advancedperipherals/patchouli_books/manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/fuel_consuming.json",
          "text": "All world changing operations will consume turtle fuel (of course, if you not disable fuel usage in CC:Tweaked configuration).Also, most of this operations have cooldowns, so you should consider this in your code. Hopefully, every active cooldown can be recived via peripheral methods.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Cooldowns and fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "advancedperipherals:manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/fuel_consuming",
          "source": "jar:AdvancedPeripherals-1.20.1-0.7.46r.jar!assets/advancedperipherals/patchouli_books/manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/fuel_consuming.json",
          "text": "You think, that cooldowns are too big? This is when fuel consuming rate come to help!Bigger fuel consuming rate will reduce cooldown, but obviously increate fuel consumption. For example, if click operation required 1 fuel point for perform and will have 5 seconds cooldown, with fuel consumption 2 you can perform click operation one in 2.5 seconds, but in cost of 2 fuel point.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Cooldowns and fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "advancedperipherals:manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/fuel_consuming",
          "source": "jar:AdvancedPeripherals-1.20.1-0.7.46r.jar!assets/advancedperipherals/patchouli_books/manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/fuel_consuming.json",
          "text": "However, fuel consumption rate is not so simple! Every automata core has max fuel consumption limitation, that can be retrieved via getConfiguration method.Also, fuel point will grow faster, than cooldown drops. Fuel consumption 3 will required 4 fuel points, fuel consumption 4 will required fuel points, etc.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Cooldowns and fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "advancedperipherals:manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/intro",
          "source": "jar:AdvancedPeripherals-1.20.1-0.7.46r.jar!assets/advancedperipherals/patchouli_books/manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/intro.json",
          "text": "This world hold many secrets from you. You feel, that even simple turtle can become a powerful tool to control reality, you just need to put something real inside it. Something more like ... soul?",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Intro",
          "item_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "advancedperipherals:manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/overpowered_automata_core",
          "source": "jar:AdvancedPeripherals-1.20.1-0.7.46r.jar!assets/advancedperipherals/patchouli_books/manual/en_us/entries/metaphysics/overpowered_automata_core.json",
          "text": "Overpowered automata core",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Overpowered automata core",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 3
        }
      ],
      "aliases": [],
      "description": "Animal products, livestock supplies, feed, hides, wool, milk, and eggs."
    },

      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Herbs and Spices",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "In Firmalife, there are a number of small plants you can collect and grow on your own, which have cooking properties. The easiest way to obtain these plants is with a Seed Ball. To use a Seed Ball, just to throw it, like a snowball. This will spawn Butterfly Grass in the area.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "The recipe for the seed ball requires Compost and 4 Seeds.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "A butterfly grass plant.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Butterfly grass will mature over time. When one reaches maturity, it has a chance to spread to surrounding blocks, or turn into something new. Butterfly grass blocks that have been spread by another grass block do not spread anymore.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Basil is one of the plants that can be spawned by butterfly grass.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Basil leaves are used in pizza.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 6
        },
        {

          "label": "Basil",

        },
        {

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",

        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "In Firmalife, there are a number of small plants you can collect and grow on your own, which have cooking properties. They can all be found in the wild, and once harvested, may be cultivated in a Greenhouse, in which they consume 20 points of N, P, and K.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Wild Basil grows between 12 and 25C, and 300 to 500mm of rainfall.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Bay Laurel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.name",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Wild Bay Laurel grows between 8 and 14C, and 25 to 225mm of rainfall.",

          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },

        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Seed Balls can be used to plant different types of Grass. This can be useful after clearing your base for building. The recipe for the seed ball requires Compost and 4 Seeds.",
