        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "There are many, many, many different types of plants in TerraFirmaCraft. Different plants appear in different Climates, and their appearance may change over the current season - going through cycles of flowering and laying dormant, or changing color as the local temperature changes. Colorful flowers can typically be crushed in a Quern for Dye.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Standard plants are like small flowers. They grow on grass, dirt, and farmland.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Dry plants are like standard plants, but they can grow on sand. These generally only spawn in areas with low rainfall.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Cacti can grow two blocks high, and they will damage you!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Creeping plants take the shape of the surrounding block faces. They mostly spawn in blobs on the ground",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Epiphytes only live on the sides of logs.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Short Grass",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.name",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Short grass blocks grow taller with age. They also are able to grow on peat and mud.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Tall Grass",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.name",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Tall grass blocks are just tall enough to block your field of view.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Vine blocks spread around the world on their own, if it's warm enough.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },

          "text": "Weeping Vines",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.name",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Weeping vines grow downward from an anchoring block.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Twisting Vines",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.name",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Twisting vines twist upward from the Earth. They can come in a solid variant.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Water plants are restricted to either spawn in fresh or salty water. Otherwise, they grow and act much like the plants you see on the surface. Some water plants can be cooked for food.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Water Plants",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.title",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Standard Water",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.name",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Standard water plants can be broken with a Knife to get Seaweed.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Water Grass",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.name",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Water grasses are grasses that grow under the water.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Tall Water Plant",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.name",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Tall water plants can grow with just the bottom block in water. Water Taro and Cattail can be broken with a Knife for Roots.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Floating plants sit on top of the water. Boats will break them on contact.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.15.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Kelp are twisting vines that grow underwater.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.16.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Tree Kelp",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.17.name",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Tree kelp grow into intricate trees underwater. The flowers can be harvested with a Knife.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.17.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "tfc:world/dune",

        },
        {

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/surface_kaolin",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/surface_kaolin.json",
          "text": "Kaolin Clay",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Kaolin Clay",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/surface_kaolin",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/surface_kaolin.json",
          "text": "Kaolin Clay is required to make Fire Clay. It can be found spawning between Y=75-110 in Plateaus, Rolling Hills and Highlands (and in their Karst variants), Old Mountains, and Shield Volcanoes at a temperature of at least 18°C, with a rainfall of at least 300mm. The Blood Lily flower grows on Kaolin clay, which can be up to 35 blocks below.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Kaolin Clay",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/surface_kaolin",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/surface_kaolin.json",
          "text": "Generally, go South East to increase temperature and humidity.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Kaolin Clay",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/surface_kaolin",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/surface_kaolin.json",
          "text": "Only 20% of Kaolin Clay will turn into Kaolinite Powder, so it's recommended to bake it on-site using Pit Kilns to save inventory space. Bring home around 20 Blocks of Kaolin Clay as well, for Glassworking later.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Kaolin Clay",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "count": 3
        },
        {

          "label": "Piglins",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/piglins",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/piglins.json",
          "text": "- 4-6 Sulfur Powder - 1-2 Leather - 6-12 String - 6-12 Nether Quartz - 12-18 Mangrove Lumber - 6-8 Mangrove Logs - 4-6 Cooked Venison - 4-6 Ghost Peppers - 4-6 Gunpowder - 4-8 Charcoal - 6-12 Arrows - 6-12 Flux - 8-12 Pyroxenite Bricks - 8-12 Migmatite Bricks - 8-16 Sticks",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Piglins",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/piglins",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/piglins.json",
          "text": "Raiding a Bastion might be very dangerous, but they can also be very rewarding! You can find things like: - Gold, Rose Gold, Pig Iron, Black Bronze, and Silver ingots - Black Bronze weapons, tools, and armor - Kaolin clay and graphite - Lanterns - Cloth, leather, seeds, and food - And other treasures!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Piglins",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/piglins",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/piglins.json",
          "text": "Piglin Disguise",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.title",
          "label": "Piglins",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/piglins",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/piglins.json",
          "text": "If you want to ensure an easy time trading--or simply mingling with the locals--Put on a Piglin Disguise to prevent Piglins from being hostile with you on first sight. But beware! They only put up with so much...",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Piglins",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "minecraft:gold_ingot",
          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "Au",
          "key": "runtime-tooltip",
          "label": "Gold Ingot",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "minecraft:gold_ingot",
          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "Melts into 144 mB of Gold (at Orange٭٭٭)",
          "key": "runtime-tooltip",
          "label": "Gold Ingot",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians.json",
          "text": "Throughout the moon, There are large, inhabited underground villages. These villages contain an alien form of the earth villager, who call themselves Lunarians. Unlike their earth counterpart, Lunarian don't need oxygen and can survive the chilling environment of the moon.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Lunarians",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians.json",
          "text": "Lunarian have different trades than earth villagers, featuring planet-specific materials, space suits and oxygen gear.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Lunarians",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians.json",
          "text": "Wandering Trader",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Lunarians",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians.json",
          "text": "Lunarian Wandering Traders appear on every moon and planet. They have many rocket-related trades that may save your life, including launch pads, oxygen, fuel, soul torches and water. They accept emeralds, which are commonly found in the various structures on the moon and planets.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Lunarians",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians.json",
          "text": "Wandering Trader",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.name",
          "label": "Lunarians",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/rover",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/rover.json",
          "text": "The rover is a land vehicle capable of driving on the rough terrain of moons and planets. It's exceptionally fast, has an inventory capable of storing 16 items and has two seats for multiple riders. You can also run over mobs and deal some serious damage. To use it, fill the rover with fuel (shift-click and place fuel buckets in the left input slot).",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Rover",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/rover",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/rover.json",
          "text": "Rover Recipe",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Rover",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/rover",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/rover.json",
          "text": "The rover has a built-in radio for playing real internet streamed radio stations. Use it by interacting with the radio on the rover or by pressing (). If multiple players are in the rover, they'll hear the same radio as well.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Rover",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Animal Husbandry",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Livestock are animals that can be tamed and bred by the player. Livestock can be either male or female. For some animals, it is possible to tell their sex visually. For example, male pigs have tusks.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Livestock experience aging. They are born as babies, which are smaller and cannot provide things for the player. After a certain number of days, they grow into adult animals, which are able to do things like breed or produce milk. After they breed or are used enough times, animals become old, and are only useful for their meat.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "This bull is old and cannot breed, so it has a faded coat and grey, unseeing eyes.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Livestock can be fed to raise familiarity. Each animal has foods that it prefers to eat. To feed an animal, and with food in your hand.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Livestock have a Familiarity Indicator that shows how familiar they are with you. Hold and look at the animal to reveal it.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Familiarity decays a little each day if not fed. Raising the familiarity enough prevents decay, indicated by the white outlined heart.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Adult livestock cannot be fully familiarized, indicated by the red outlined heart. Babies can reach 100% familiarity.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Mammals are livestock that experience Pregnancy. Adult mammals that are above 30% familiarity and have been fed that day will mate, given that they are of opposite genders and near each other. The female animal will become pregnant, which causes it to have children a set number of days after fertilization. An example is Pigs.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Wooly Animals are Mammals that can be Sheared if they are adults and familiar enough to you. Some examples are Sheep, Alpacas, and Musk Oxen.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Dairy Animals are mammals that make Milk. Female dairy animals can be clicked with a bucket to obtain milk. Some examples are Goats, Cows, and Yaks.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Nest Box",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.title",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Oviparous Animals are not Mammals, and instead produce children by laying Eggs. They need a Nest Box to lay eggs, which they are capable of locating on their own. Some examples are Ducks, Quails, and Chickens.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Eggs can be cooked or boiled for food. Male oviparous animals can fertilize females, which causes the next egg laid in the nest box to be fertilized. Fertilized eggs will have a tooltip with how long until they are ready to hatch.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Equines are Mammals that can be ridden when tamed. They become rideable after reaching 15% familiarity.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "They need a Saddle to ride, which can be Knapped. This includes Mules, Donkeys, and Horses. Mules and Donkeys can hold any chest or barrel. If holding a barrel, while holding can be used to remove it. The same keys while holding a bucket can be used to drain fluid from the barrel. The next few pages will go over all livestock types.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Pigs spawn in mild forests with temperature between -10 and 35°C, and at least 200mm of rainfall. They are Mammals with no special abilities. They will eat any food, even if it is rotten. They have 1-10 children, are pregnant for just 19 days, and reach adulthood in 80 days. They can have children 6 times.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "A pig.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.15.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Cows spawn in most climates, between temperature -10 and 35°C, and at least 250mm of rainfall. They are Dairy Animals. They only eat grains, which may be rotten. They can have 1-2 children, are pregnant for 58 days, and reach adulthood in 192 days. They can have children 13 times, if they are never milked, or be milked 128 times, if they are never bred. They produce milk every day.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.16.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "A cow.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.17.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Goats spawn in moderate climates, with temperature between -12 and 25°C, and at least 300mm of rainfall. They are Dairy Animals. They eat grains, fruits, and vegetables, which may be rotten. They can have 1-2 children, are pregnant for 32 days, and reach adulthood in 96 days. They can have children 6 times if they are never milked, or be milked 60 times if they are never bred. They produce milk every 3 days.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.18.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "A goat.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.19.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Yaks spawn in cold climates, with temperature of at most -11°C, and at least 100mm of rainfall. They are Dairy Animals. They eat only fresh grains. They always have 1 child, are pregnant for 64 days, and reach adulthood in 180 days. They can have children 23 times, if they are never milked, or be milked 230 times, if they are never bred. They produce milk once a day.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.20.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "A yak.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.21.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",

          "text": "Chickens spawn in warm forests, with temperature of at least 14°C, and at least 225mm of rainfall. They are Oviparous Animals.           "text": "Ducks spawn in most plains, with temperature between -25 and 30°C, and at least 100mm of rainfall. They are Oviparous Animals.           "text": "Quails spawn in colder climates, with temperature between -15 and 15°C, and at least 200mm of rainfall. They are Oviparous Animals.           "text": "Donkeys spawn in wetter plains, with temperature of at least -15°C, and between 130 and 400mm of rainfall. They are a kind of Equine that can carry a chest.           "text": "Mules spawn in plains with temperature of at least -15°C, and between 130 and 400mm of rainfall. They are a kind of Equine that can carry a chest and are the always-male product of a horse and a donkey. They eat grains and fruits. They reach adulthood in 80 days.",

          "text": "Horses spawn in plains with temperature of at least -15°C, and between 130 and 400mm of rainfall. They are a kind of Equine. They eat grains and fruits. They have 1 child, are pregnant for 19 days, and reach adulthood in 80 days. They can have children 6 times.",

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/pets",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/pets.json",
          "text": "Pets are animals that function quite similar to Livestock, but have no useful products. Instead, they can obey commands, follow you around your home, control pests, and help you hunt.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Pets",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/pets",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/pets.json",
          "text": "Pets recognize a certain player as their Owner. To become a pet's owner, feed it to raise its familiarity above 15%%. Pets will only obey commands that come from their owner. Clicking on a pet with dye changes its collar color.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Pets",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 10
        }

      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops.json",
          "text": "Extraterrestrial Crops",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops.json",
          "text": "Extraterrestrial Crops",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops.json",
          "text": "It turns out Earth isn't the only celestial body with life. During your travels, you may come across other kinds of edible flora. These all use the same mechanics you're used to on Earth (with some exceptions), and can be grown either in normal Farmland, in a Firmalife Greenhouse, or in a GregTech Electric Greenhouse depending on your needs.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops.json",
          "text": "An Air Distributor will also keep an enclosed area a stable 15 °C. Due to technical reasons, this temperature can't be displayed in the Jade tooltip while on other planets, so you'll have to use your inventory's Climate tab to check the temperature. Another alternative is to use Firmalife's Greenhouse, which doesn't provide as much output, but also ignores all climate conditions.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops.json",
          "text": "- Amber Root - Blossom Berry - Bolux Mushroom - Bulbkin - Chalmie Mushroom - Chorus Fruit - Nox Berry",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops.json",
          "text": "Chorus Fruit",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops.json",
          "text": "Location: The Moon Biomes: Chorus Forest, Chorus Thicket Chorus Plants grow in tall formations. Breaking the stem will yield nothing, while breaking the Flowers at the top will yield Chorus Fruits or a Flower. Chorus Fruits can be eaten for Fruit, cooked and eaten as Popped Chorus Fruit, or can be distilled into Nitrogen.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops.json",
          "text": "Chorus Flowers can also be grown manually by planting them on any Gravel or Sand.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops.json",
          "text": "Amber Root",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.title",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops.json",
          "text": "Location: Mars Temperature: -150 - 15 °C Hydration: 0 - 40 % Nutrient: Phosphorus Amber root is a single block crop. Amber seeds can be planted on farmland and will produce Amber Shoots which can be eaten for Grain.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops.json",
          "text": "Wild Amber Root grows in any climate on Amber and Rusticus Mycelium.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops.json",
          "text": "Location: Mars Temperature: -80 - 30 °C Hydration: 70 - 100 % Nutrient: Potassium Blossom Berry is a single block crop. Blossom Berry Seeds can be planted on farmland and will produce Blossom Berries which can be eaten for Fruit.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops.json",
          "text": "Blossom Berry",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.title",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops.json",
          "text": "Wild Blossom Berry grows in any climate on Amber, Rusticus, or Sangnum Mycelium.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops.json",
          "text": "Location: Mars Temperature: -150 - 15 °C Hydration: 0 - 40 % Nutrient: Phosphorus Bolux Mushrooms are a single block crop. Bolux Mushroom Spores can be planted on farmland and will produce Bolux Mushrooms which can be eaten for Vegetables. It is recommended to cook Bolux Mushrooms before eating.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 18
        },
        {

        {

          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops.json",
          "text": "Wild Bolux Mushrooms grow in any climate on Amber, Rusticus, or Sangnum Mycelium.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_crops.json",
          "text": "Location: Mars Temperature: -100 - 30 °C Hydration: 0 - 60 % Nutrient: Nitrogen Bulbkins are a spreading crop. Bulbkin Seeds can be planted on farmland and will place up to two Bulbkin Blocks on the ground next to it while it is mature. If the bulbkin blocks are harvested, and the plant matures again, it can grow more bulbkins. Bulbkins can be eaten for Fruit or processed into Glowstone Dust.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "count": 18

        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Clay is an incredibly useful and balanced material which can be used for pottery. It can prove challenging to locate at first. Clay is usually hidden by grass, but it is often found in two locations. In areas with at least 175mm Annual Rainfall, clay can be found in patches all over the place, usually marked by the presence of certain Plants.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Clay Indicators",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.name",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Clay, with one of the plants that may indicate its presence.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Athyrium Fern, Canna, Goldenrod, Pampas Grass, Perovskia, and Water Canna all indicate the presence of clay nearby. Clay can also be found in smaller deposits close to water sources, such as rivers, lakes, or ponds. Like with rocks, clay can be knapped to form new items. It requires five clay in your hand to knap. Unlike rocks, if you make a mistake, you can simply close the knapping interface, reshape your clay, and try again.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "The Knapping Interface.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "The small vessel is one such item. Like all pottery items, it must be fired before it can be used. Firing is a process of heating the item up to a point where the clay will turn into a hard Ceramic material, which requires heating to 1400 °C, or Yellow White. In order to do this in the early game, you will need to use a Pit Kiln.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Small Vessel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Knapping a Clay Small Vessel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Another useful pottery item is the Jug. It can be used to pick up and drink fluids, such as fresh water. In order to use it, simply the jug on the fluid in the world. Then use the jug in order to drink from it. The jug can hold 100 mB of fluid at a time.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Knapping a Clay Jug.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Clay is also necessary for making Molds. Molds can have molten metal poured into them, which will eventually solidify into the shape of a mold. The item and potentially the mold can then be retrieved by using on the mold. The most simple type of mold is the ingot mold, to the right.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Knapping a Clay Ingot Mold.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "The mold then needs to be fired, like all clay items, to be usable - likely in a Pit Kiln. Once it is fired, molten metal can be poured in. Once the metal has cooled enough, it can be extracted.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "The next few pages show several of the knapping patterns for various tools.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Prospector's Pick is an essential tool for locating large quantities of ore.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Pickaxe! The bread and butter tool for mining.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Saw is a tool which is required in order to craft advanced wooden components like a Workbench along with many other devices like Supports.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Scythe is a tool that can harvest plants and leaves in a 3x3x3 area!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.15.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Chisel is a tool used for smoothing blocks as well as creating a large number of decorative blocks.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.16.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "An Axe for all your tree chopping purposes. Note that stone axes are less efficient than metal!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.17.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Hammer is an essential tool to create and work on Anvils.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.18.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Knife can be used as a weapon, or as a cutting tool for plant type blocks.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.19.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Hoe used for planting and maintaining Crops.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.20.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Shovel for all your digging purposes.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.21.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_pit",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_pit.json",
          "text": "Charcoal Pit",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Charcoal Pit",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_pit",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_pit.json",
          "text": "The Charcoal Pit is a way of obtaining Charcoal. Charcoal pits are made with Log Piles. To place a log pile, and while holding a Log. More logs can be inserted by either pressing directly while holding a log, or by pressing with something else to open the interface.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Charcoal Pit",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_pit",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_pit.json",
          "text": "The Log Pile",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.name",
          "label": "Charcoal Pit",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_pit",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_pit.json",
          "text": "Log piles need a solid block under them to be placed. They are highly flammable.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Charcoal Pit",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_pit",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_pit.json",
          "text": "The charcoal pit is formed by surrounding log piles with solid, non-flammable blocks. The amount of charcoal produced is proportional to the amount of logs contained inside the log piles. To start the burning process, light one of the log piles, and then cover it. If it worked, you should see smoke particles rise up from the structure.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Charcoal Pit",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_pit",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_pit.json",
          "text": "The building of one possible charcoal pit, in layers.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Charcoal Pit",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_pit",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_pit.json",
          "text": "After the charcoal pit burns out and stops smoking, you will be left with Charcoal piles. The charcoal pile contains up to 8 layers of Charcoal. Dig it with a shovel to obtain the charcoal items. Charcoal piles can be added to or placed with .",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Charcoal Pit",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_pit",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_pit.json",
          "text": "The charcoal pile.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Charcoal Pit",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_crops.json",
          "text": "Beneath Crops",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Beneath Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_crops.json",
          "text": "Beneath Crops",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Beneath Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_crops.json",
          "text": "The Beneath may be a harsh environment, but it is still possible to find crops there. Beneath crops are incredibly resilient and can grow in extreme heat conditions making them useful for surviving below the Earth and on the surface.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Beneath Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_crops.json",
          "text": "Crops in the beneath behave just the same as crops on the surface, they have hydration, temperature, and nutrient requirements. You can even plant them in Greenhouses. An important concern when planting crops in the Beneath is that crops require a light level of 12, which isnt provided by the Beneath's natural light. To solve this problem, you can utilize Lava Aqueducts or any other available light source.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Beneath Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 12
        }
      ],
      "aliases": [],
      "description": "Harvested crops and field produce."
    },
