      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "create:table_cloth_shop",
          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!data/create/advancements/table_cloth_shop.json",
          "text": "Open for business",
          "key": "advancement-title:title",
          "label": "Open for business",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "create:table_cloth_shop",
          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!data/create/advancements/table_cloth_shop.json",
          "text": "Put items up for sale using a Table Cloth",
          "key": "advancement-description:description",
          "label": "Open for business",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "create_logistics",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "By utilising a few machines from Create, you'll be able to improve your item transportation and storage capabilities significantly. Later on once you progress into the Voltaic Age, you'll even be able to set up processes to automatically order specific items or route them around your base as needed!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "As this questline deals primarily with machines from Create, this means Pondering the machines will be your best source of information aside from these quests, so be sure to look through their entries.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Boxing Clever",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "all_started",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "When provided with a redstone signal, the Packager will wrap up items from the block it is attached to (you can switch the connection side with a wrench) and output a neat little cardboard package. Each cardboard package has 9 slots' worth of inventory, so it's great for moving a bunch of items around at once. To craft the Packager, you need plenty of Wrought Iron and an Electron Tube, not to mention a bunch of Cardboard (Paper and Glue).",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Note that once crafted, the packager works without any additional cardboard, so don't worry about upkeep!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "{@pagebreak}",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Putting a Sign on the Packager will make it automatically label the sign's contents onto all packages as a destination address. This will be more useful once you start working with various ways to route packages around.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Packagers can also unwrap any packages that get put into it, unloading their contents into the attached inventory.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Note that Framed Signs do not work for this purpose!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Pack it up",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Packages can be routed using the specialized Package Filter, which can be used just like any filter in any Create machine that supports filter slots. Just right click while holding the filter and type in the address that should be filtered, and any packages with that specified destination address will be let through. This is useful especially for Brass Tunnels to make your belt network route your packages around based on destination.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "The general solution to package routing",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Package Filters",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Package Frogports are one of three ways you can move packages around and get them to where you want them to be. They can hang packages onto Chain Conveyor lines be transported along the chain connections. To use them, you first need a network of Chain Conveyors connected to each other using chains and then powered: Any chain with the tag #forge:chains will do. Right click on a chain connection before placing down a Frogport to have it interact with that segment of chain.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Frogports placed directly on top of a Packager automatically hang up packages that are created in said Packager.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Frogports can also receive packages in addition to sending them. If you right click on a Frogport, you can see that it has a name you can edit. This name acts as an address - if any packages that are labelled with a destination address matches its name, the Frogport will automatically collect them by taking them off the chain network! This collected package will be put into the inventory of the block the Frogport is placed on.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "As long as the chain network is connected to the input and output Frogports somehow, the package will eventually find its way, although you may want to optimize the paths to reduce package travel time on your own.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "The chain of command",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Package Frogports",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Postboxes are used to route packages around using Train Networks. You first right click a Train Station to link the Postbox to that station before placing it, and from then on any trains that stop there will be able to interact with the Postbox (given it is within 4 blocks of the station).",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Much like Package Frogports, they can be named. Passing trains will then pick up any packages whose destination does not match the Postbox's name. Furthermore, if the train has any packages in its inventories that do match the Postbox, they will be passed into the Postbox's inventory instead.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "One last cool thing about Postboxes is that when extracting automatically from them (via chutes, funnels, etc), only packages with a destination that matches the Postbox's name will be taken out and any packages with a different destination will remain inside to be loaded onto trains later.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "You've got mail",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Here are some other important things that may be useful when trying to route packages around:",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "- Packages are randomly created from a selection of four possible items, so stuff like GregTech's Item Filters will be very tedious to set up! We recommend using the Package Filter wherever you can, but...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "- If you really want to try using other methods to move packages around, the destination address is stored as NBT data in the tag Address. For example, a package with the destination address \"Home\" will have in its NBT data: {Address:\"Home\"}.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "- Package address filtering actually supports a simplified form of RegEx. This means you can:",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "- Match any length string with *",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "- Match any single character with ?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "- Match a single character from a group of characters surrounded by [] (e.g. A[cr]t will match 'Act' and 'Art', but not 'Ant')",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "- Match a single character that does not belong to a group of characters surrounded by [! ] (e.g. A[!cr]t will match 'Aft' and 'Apt', but not 'Act' or 'Art')",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "- Match a string from a list of strings given inside {}(e.g. TerraFirma{Craft,Greg} will match both TerraFirmaCraft and TerraFirmaGreg)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "- All previous symbols can be escaped; For example, A\\* will be read as A* and only match 'A*'.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Other information on packages and addresses",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Other Information",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Tell me more",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "one_completed",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Whichever way you obtained the materials needed, you need a Lathe or Belt Grinder to actually create the Lens. Either a Basic Lathe or Belt Grinder will be enough to create the lens from an Exquisite Rose Quartz gem, but if you want to make the lens from a mere plate you will need better equipment in the form of an Advanced Lathe.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Once you've crafted it, the lens can be used in a Basic Laser Engraver with a Double Red Alloy Plate to create the Transmitter unit you need, and won't be consumed in the process.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Note: Once you make it to MV, you'll be able to make more Rose Quartz at home - just be sure to reserve at least one Rose Quartz Gem for it!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "YONO (You Only Need One)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Rose-tinted Glasses",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Moving packages around is good and all, but surely that can't be all there is? Well, you're exactly right. There's much more that can be done with packages, including reading inventory contents and having things be delivered remotely, and later on even automatically sending ingredients to be processed based on an item's threshold!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "But before delving into High Logistics, some preparations are in order.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "The Transmitters that would be required for crafting such equipment can only realistically be produced via high-energy light refracted through a very unique mineral: Rose Quartz. In other words, we need both a Basic Laser Engraver and some way of obtaining a Rose Quartz Lens, so you better get to getting these done, eh?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "...But Rose Quartz is Pink",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Roses are red...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "one_started",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "The only natural source of Rose Quartz of a high enough quality to easily craft the Lens out of is located far, far to the north in freezing temperatures (below -10°C). Getting Kaolin clay flashbacks yet?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Rose Quartz only forms in the cores of ancient glaciers. Although not common, the glacier core is easily identified by their Blue Ice outer shells. The Rose Quartz will be found inside - first as ore, then as compressed raw ore blocks in the very center.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "What we need is Exquisite Rose Quartz, which you can get by crushing, washing, and then sifting the raw ore you mined; The Exquisite gem has a 3 percent chance of being sifted out, but you only need one such gem to proceed.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Do not smelt the ore as this will only get you normal quality Rose Quartz!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "The same preparations you once may have made for Kaolin clay will apply here as well: Prepare a lot of food, a source of portable water to drink, and some transportation (boats are nice, but aircraft should be well within your reach at this point).",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "If you created your world prior to TFG 0.12 or are using the older worldgen, rose quartz can be found in low elevation biomes such as the Ocean, Shores, and Lowlands. Otherwise, if you're using the new worldgen, you'll want to look for Ice Sheets and Glaciers.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "The North Remembers",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Finding Rose Quartz",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "The first High Logistics machine you can make is a Stock Link, which can be used to create a Logistics Network. When placed onto any side of a Packager, the Stock Link will make the inventory the Packager is attached to visible to the network! On its own, the Stock Link isn't super useful, but it is a core component of all the other machines yet to come...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Remember to right click on an existing Stock Link before placing one down to tune the two to share the same network. To cancel this, you can simply craft the Stock Link by itself to clear its attunement.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "We got storage buses at home",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Stock Link",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        }
      ],
      "aliases": [
        "cloth",
        "textiles"
      ],
      "description": "Cloth, fabric, thread, string, weaving, and sewing supplies."
    },
    {
      "id": "pack:tfg/pig_food",

      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "ad_astra:moon_cheese",

          "text": "Not FDA Approved",
          "key": "advancement-title:title",
          "label": "Not FDA Approved",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "ad_astra:moon_cheese",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!data/ad_astra/advancements/moon_cheese.json",
          "text": "Obtain moon cheese. I wonder where it came from?",
          "key": "advancement-description:description",
          "label": "Not FDA Approved",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "queststfc_tips",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "This quest chapter gives you a lot, (and we do mean a lot) of tips on how to survive and thrive.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Each quest branch representing major mods in TerraFirmaGreg, with tips to make the most out of them. None of these quests are necessary for progression, although some may be locked behind main questline tasks.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Dear God that's a lot of branches...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Tips and Tricks be here!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Firmalife is a mod all about extending the agricultural and gastronomic experience in TerraFirmaCraft, with touches of miscellaneous features. Includes things such as decorations, more preservation methods, new foods, bees and a plethora of useful gadgets!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Thrive in TerraFirmaCraft",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "ArborFirmaCraft is a mod that adds multiple new trees to TFG: some are variants of existing TFC trees, such as Ancient Kapok, while others are completely new, such as the Baobab and Hevea. Some of the plank and log textures of these trees may remind you of vanilla tree colors...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "This section will go over Tree Tapping.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "As a bonus, all ArborFirmaCraft woods are compatible with FirmaLife, and Firma:Civ, we made sure of it!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:arborfirmacraft/tapping_index",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Harvesting the Life-blood of Trees",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Deep, DEEP underground, past the Bedrock, you'll find The Beneath, a complex network of huge caves filled with dangers and strange flora and fauna. While The Beneath is completely optional, it contains a lot of resources as well as some quality of life improvements.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:beneath/beneath",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Journey to the Center of the Earth",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "The Beneath",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "TerraFirmaCraft is one of the main mods in TerraFirmaGreg. This branch mostly focuses on some of its survival features and other Add-Ons the modpack has, such as Aged Alcohol and Canes.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "The other two thirds of the modpack's name",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "TerraFirmaCraft Addons",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "To begin tapping trees, you'll need to forge a Tree Tap from working Copper Ingots on an Anvil.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "The process of tapping requires finding a suitable tree, a dedicated Tapping Index in the ArborFirmaCraft Field Guide section will tell you how to find these trees. There's also the Tapping Trees entry, which can be used to learn how to actually tap them.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:arborfirmacraft/tree_tapping",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Now with less jank!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "one_completed",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Utilizing the Vacuum Chamber and some additional heating, you can process Latex from various trees into Raw Rubber Pulp through a specific process. You can then smelt together 3 Raw Rubber Pulp with a bit of Sulfur Dust in an Alloy Smelter to create Rubber Ingots, perfect for insulating cables and very much necessary to advance in the Voltaic Age and beyond...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Tip: As you progress, there will be several ways to make this process cheaper and easier. Keep checking EMI! You'll be using Rubber for a long time, so it's well worth automating.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Insulate them cables",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Rubber Ingot",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Sticky Resin can be utilized as slime balls in some recipes. It is also key in the production of Resin Circuits and Resistors, which you'll need a fair bit of to advance into the Low Voltage age.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "To make sticky resin, you need to boil either Latex or Conifer Pitch in a Vat mixed with Wood Ash.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "No, it's not Rosin",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Sticky Resin",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Latex Trees can be found in temperate areas of the world that receive large amounts of rainfall. It can be tapped to obtain Latex, used to create both Sticky Resin, and Vulcanized Latex. The latter is used to produce Rubber and Rubber Gloves, which protect you from poisonous materials on contact.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "To complete this quest, you need to look at a block that has the tag #tfg:latex_logs. You can use EMI to learn which blocks have this tag by searching with that hashtag.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:arborfirmacraft/making_rubber",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Seek for the Tropics",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "ftbfiltersystem:item_tag(tfg:latex_logs)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Any #tfg:latex_logs",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Latex Trees",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Syrup Trees can be found in temperate areas of the world that receive moderate amounts of rainfall. It can be tapped to obtain Syrup. Syrup can be used to create Tree Sugars, an alternative sugar among other uses.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "To complete this quest, you need to look at a block that has the tag #tfg:syrup_logs. You can use EMI to learn which blocks have this tag.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:arborfirmacraft/making_syrups",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Diabetes is Skyrocketing",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",

          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "#tfg:syrup_logs",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Syrup Trees",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Tree Sugar is a sugar substitute, mostly used as a food ingredient.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "To make it, you'll need to boil Tree Sap to get Concentrated Sap, then boil it again to get Syrup. Finally, use a workbench to turn a Bucket of Syrup into Tree sugar.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Keep in mind that all these boiling processes require a stick in the Vat or pot.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Sweet!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(afc:maple_sugar)item(afc:birch_sugar))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Maple or Birch Sugar",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Tree Sugar",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "If you have advanced to the MV age, you can make a Refrigerator, consuming power in exchange for the best universal food preservation trait for all food items. This includes raw and cooked food, and combined foods like sandwiches.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "This thing may be overpowered",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Beekeeping has a plethora of uses. Not only do bees fertilize nearby crops (assuming you have the correct trait), you will be able to obtain useful resources such as Wax and Honey. Breed bees to achieve better stats.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "The Field Guide offers an extensive explanation of all beekeeping mechanics.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:firmalife/beekeeping",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "They're so small now...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Click here to complete this quest/task",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Bees don't like it when people disrupt them and harvest their precious honey and wax or move their frames around. There are three ways to do it safely:",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "* Harvesting at night time",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],

    {

      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "ad_astra:moon_cheese",

          "text": "Not FDA Approved",
          "key": "advancement-title:title",
          "label": "Not FDA Approved",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "ad_astra:moon_cheese",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!data/ad_astra/advancements/moon_cheese.json",
          "text": "Obtain moon cheese. I wonder where it came from?",
          "key": "advancement-description:description",
          "label": "Not FDA Approved",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "queststfc_tips",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "This quest chapter gives you a lot, (and we do mean a lot) of tips on how to survive and thrive.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Each quest branch representing major mods in TerraFirmaGreg, with tips to make the most out of them. None of these quests are necessary for progression, although some may be locked behind main questline tasks.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Dear God that's a lot of branches...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Tips and Tricks be here!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Firmalife is a mod all about extending the agricultural and gastronomic experience in TerraFirmaCraft, with touches of miscellaneous features. Includes things such as decorations, more preservation methods, new foods, bees and a plethora of useful gadgets!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Thrive in TerraFirmaCraft",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "ArborFirmaCraft is a mod that adds multiple new trees to TFG: some are variants of existing TFC trees, such as Ancient Kapok, while others are completely new, such as the Baobab and Hevea. Some of the plank and log textures of these trees may remind you of vanilla tree colors...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "This section will go over Tree Tapping.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "As a bonus, all ArborFirmaCraft woods are compatible with FirmaLife, and Firma:Civ, we made sure of it!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:arborfirmacraft/tapping_index",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Harvesting the Life-blood of Trees",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Deep, DEEP underground, past the Bedrock, you'll find The Beneath, a complex network of huge caves filled with dangers and strange flora and fauna. While The Beneath is completely optional, it contains a lot of resources as well as some quality of life improvements.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:beneath/beneath",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Journey to the Center of the Earth",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "The Beneath",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "TerraFirmaCraft is one of the main mods in TerraFirmaGreg. This branch mostly focuses on some of its survival features and other Add-Ons the modpack has, such as Aged Alcohol and Canes.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "The other two thirds of the modpack's name",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "TerraFirmaCraft Addons",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "To begin tapping trees, you'll need to forge a Tree Tap from working Copper Ingots on an Anvil.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "The process of tapping requires finding a suitable tree, a dedicated Tapping Index in the ArborFirmaCraft Field Guide section will tell you how to find these trees. There's also the Tapping Trees entry, which can be used to learn how to actually tap them.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:arborfirmacraft/tree_tapping",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Now with less jank!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "one_completed",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Utilizing the Vacuum Chamber and some additional heating, you can process Latex from various trees into Raw Rubber Pulp through a specific process. You can then smelt together 3 Raw Rubber Pulp with a bit of Sulfur Dust in an Alloy Smelter to create Rubber Ingots, perfect for insulating cables and very much necessary to advance in the Voltaic Age and beyond...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Tip: As you progress, there will be several ways to make this process cheaper and easier. Keep checking EMI! You'll be using Rubber for a long time, so it's well worth automating.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Insulate them cables",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Rubber Ingot",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Sticky Resin can be utilized as slime balls in some recipes. It is also key in the production of Resin Circuits and Resistors, which you'll need a fair bit of to advance into the Low Voltage age.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "To make sticky resin, you need to boil either Latex or Conifer Pitch in a Vat mixed with Wood Ash.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "No, it's not Rosin",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Latex Trees can be found in temperate areas of the world that receive large amounts of rainfall. It can be tapped to obtain Latex, used to create both Sticky Resin, and Vulcanized Latex. The latter is used to produce Rubber and Rubber Gloves, which protect you from poisonous materials on contact.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "To complete this quest, you need to look at a block that has the tag #tfg:latex_logs. You can use EMI to learn which blocks have this tag by searching with that hashtag.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:arborfirmacraft/making_rubber",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",

        {

          "key": "quest-snbt",

          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Latex Trees",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Syrup Trees can be found in temperate areas of the world that receive moderate amounts of rainfall. It can be tapped to obtain Syrup. Syrup can be used to create Tree Sugars, an alternative sugar among other uses.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "To complete this quest, you need to look at a block that has the tag #tfg:syrup_logs. You can use EMI to learn which blocks have this tag.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",

          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Diabetes is Skyrocketing",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
