        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Go and start working on those Low Voltage components!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Dear God...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "This... is a Bucket.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The final task in the Metallurgy Age is creating a Bucket. Unlike the wooden bucket and other fluid containers you've been using until now, the Bucket can be used to carry Lava and move around source blocks of other fluids.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Bucket Fusion Reactor",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Bucket Components",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "With the Black Steel Anvil you'll now be able to work Black Steel Ingots into different items.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "You've also now opened up a massive new section in the Early Automation chapter related to high pressure Steam Power!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "From here, you can either dive straight into industrialization or finish up the last tier of anvils if you'd like. You can't avoid colored steel forever, though!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Steam power is within your grasp",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "T5 Anvil: Black Steel",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "With the Steel Anvil, you'll be able to work Steel Ingots into different forms.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Steel tools and equipment have a significant boost over their bronze and wrought iron counterparts, so they're worth making if your existing ones are about to break. You'll be needing to create a lot of alloys soon, so some new mining equipment would be a wise investment.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:mechanics/steel",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "A huge leap from wrought iron",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "T4 Anvil: Steel",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "With the Wrought Iron Anvil you'll be able to work Wrought Iron Ingots into different items such as Sheets, Tool heads, etc.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Wrought Iron tools and armor isn't much of an improvement over Bronze, but it has far more durability.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Creating the next anvil is going to be a significant step up in difficulty, so this is a good point to settle down and explore some of the other things this modpack has to offer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Waterwheels, Windmills and more Greate",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "T3 Anvil: Wrought Iron",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "To work metal into more than just double ingots, you'll need to use an anvil of the same tier or better. Working on an anvil can be a little bit complicated, but there's an entry in the TFG Tips section of the field guide that goes into more detail.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Bronze also marks the start of automation, where you'll begin to harness mechanical power!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Basic mechanical power starts here",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "ftbfiltersystem:item_tag(tfc:bronze_anvils)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.tasktype.item.any} #tfc:bronze_anvils",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "T2 Anvil: Bronze",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Obtain at least 14 Ingots of any Bronze type, then weld them together in the Copper Anvil.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Tier 2 Anvil can be made from Bismuth Bronze, Bronze or Black Bronze, but not a mixture of the three.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "I told you to get used to it",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(gtceu:bronze_double_ingot)item(gtceu:black_bronze_double_ingot)item(gtceu:bismuth_bronze_double_ingot))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.tasktype.item.any} #forge:double_ingots/bronze",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Welding Bronze",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Bloomery is a Multiblock from TerraFirmaCraft that allows you to create Wrought Iron. Melting iron in a forge will produce Cast Iron, which is brittle and nowhere near as strong or durable as Wrought Iron.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Bloomery is expensive, so it will be worth investing some time into setting up automatic plate production and ore processing.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Field Guide contains a detailed entry on how the Bloomery works and the correct ratios of iron and fuel that it needs.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:mechanics/bloomery",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Required if you seek the Iron Age.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Blast Furnace is a multiblock structure that allows you to create Pig Iron. Placing a combination of Iron, Flux and fuel (see the adjacent quest) inside the Blast Furnace produces Pig Iron, which can be worked into Steel.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "In order for the Blast Furnace to reach its required high temperatures, it will need its own Bellows, used to force air through a Tuyere which you place inside the blast furnace.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Field Guide contains an entry on how the Blast Furnace works and how to build one.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Then I started Blastin'",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "ftbfiltersystem:item_tag(tfc:tuyeres)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.tasktype.item.any} #tfc:tuyeres",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Blast Furnace",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Bellows is a device that can force extra air into a fire or forge in front of it, allowing the heat produced by the fuel inside to reach higher temperatures than usual, at the cost of fuel duration.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "These higher temperatures are required to fire pottery with charcoal or melt certain metals.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Coal and Coke can also reach higher temperatures without it.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:mechanics/bellows",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Higher temperatures can be reached, at a small price.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "one_completed",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/aqueducts",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/aqueducts.json",
          "text": "Aqueducts are a tool used for moving Water around. Unlike Wooden Buckets, which are not able to transport water sources, Aqueducts are able to move source blocks horizontally any distance to their destination. They can be used to transport any type of water, including Fresh Water, Salt Water, and even Hot Spring Water.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Aqueducts",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/aqueducts",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/aqueducts.json",
          "text": "A singe aqueduct block can be crafted with some Bricks and Mortar.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Aqueducts",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/aqueducts",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/aqueducts.json",
          "text": "In order to use an Aqueduct, you must simply connect them in a horizontal pattern, and place one end adjacent to either a Source or Falling water. After waiting a short while, water will begin to flow through the aqueduct network, and out the other end. Note if aqueducts are removed, water will cease flowing, and Aqueducts will not create permanent source blocks - any water will disappear after the aqueducts are removed.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Aqueducts",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Dear God...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "This... is a Bucket.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The final task in the Metallurgy Age is creating a Bucket. Unlike the wooden bucket and other fluid containers you've been using until now, the Bucket can be used to carry Lava and move around source blocks of other fluids.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Bucket Fusion Reactor",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Bucket Components",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "With the Black Steel Anvil you'll now be able to work Black Steel Ingots into different items.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "You've also now opened up a massive new section in the Early Automation chapter related to high pressure Steam Power!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "From here, you can either dive straight into industrialization or finish up the last tier of anvils if you'd like. You can't avoid colored steel forever, though!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Steam power is within your grasp",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "T5 Anvil: Black Steel",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "With the Steel Anvil, you'll be able to work Steel Ingots into different forms.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Steel tools and equipment have a significant boost over their bronze and wrought iron counterparts, so they're worth making if your existing ones are about to break. You'll be needing to create a lot of alloys soon, so some new mining equipment would be a wise investment.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:mechanics/steel",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "A huge leap from wrought iron",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "T4 Anvil: Steel",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "With the Wrought Iron Anvil you'll be able to work Wrought Iron Ingots into different items such as Sheets, Tool heads, etc.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Wrought Iron tools and armor isn't much of an improvement over Bronze, but it has far more durability.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Creating the next anvil is going to be a significant step up in difficulty, so this is a good point to settle down and explore some of the other things this modpack has to offer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Waterwheels, Windmills and more Greate",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "T3 Anvil: Wrought Iron",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "To work metal into more than just double ingots, you'll need to use an anvil of the same tier or better. Working on an anvil can be a little bit complicated, but there's an entry in the TFG Tips section of the field guide that goes into more detail.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Bronze also marks the start of automation, where you'll begin to harness mechanical power!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Basic mechanical power starts here",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "ftbfiltersystem:item_tag(tfc:bronze_anvils)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.tasktype.item.any} #tfc:bronze_anvils",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "T2 Anvil: Bronze",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Obtain at least 14 Ingots of any Bronze type, then weld them together in the Copper Anvil.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Tier 2 Anvil can be made from Bismuth Bronze, Bronze or Black Bronze, but not a mixture of the three.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "I told you to get used to it",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(gtceu:bronze_double_ingot)item(gtceu:black_bronze_double_ingot)item(gtceu:bismuth_bronze_double_ingot))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.tasktype.item.any} #forge:double_ingots/bronze",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Welding Bronze",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Bloomery is a Multiblock from TerraFirmaCraft that allows you to create Wrought Iron. Melting iron in a forge will produce Cast Iron, which is brittle and nowhere near as strong or durable as Wrought Iron.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Bloomery is expensive, so it will be worth investing some time into setting up automatic plate production and ore processing.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Field Guide contains a detailed entry on how the Bloomery works and the correct ratios of iron and fuel that it needs.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:mechanics/bloomery",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Required if you seek the Iron Age.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Blast Furnace is a multiblock structure that allows you to create Pig Iron. Placing a combination of Iron, Flux and fuel (see the adjacent quest) inside the Blast Furnace produces Pig Iron, which can be worked into Steel.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "In order for the Blast Furnace to reach its required high temperatures, it will need its own Bellows, used to force air through a Tuyere which you place inside the blast furnace.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Field Guide contains an entry on how the Blast Furnace works and how to build one.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Then I started Blastin'",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "ftbfiltersystem:item_tag(tfc:tuyeres)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.tasktype.item.any} #tfc:tuyeres",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Blast Furnace",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Bellows is a device that can force extra air into a fire or forge in front of it, allowing the heat produced by the fuel inside to reach higher temperatures than usual, at the cost of fuel duration.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "These higher temperatures are required to fire pottery with charcoal or melt certain metals.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Coal and Coke can also reach higher temperatures without it.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:mechanics/bellows",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Higher temperatures can be reached, at a small price.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "one_completed",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],
      "aliases": [],
      "description": "Candidate player storage section inferred from pack evidence; accept only if it is a useful way to group items."
    }
  ],
  "required_output_contract": {

    "required_candidate_ids": [
      "pack:tfg/unprocessed_ores",
      "pack:tfg/dirt_and_rocks",
      "pack:tfg/refined_ores",
      "pack:tfg/decorative",
